#compdef linear

autoload -U is-at-least

_linear() {
    typeset -A opt_args
    typeset -a _arguments_options
    local ret=1

    if is-at-least 5.2; then
        _arguments_options=(-s -S -C)
    else
        _arguments_options=(-s -C)
    fi

    local context curcontext="$curcontext" state line
    _arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'-h[Print help (see more with '\''--help'\'')]' \
'--help[Print help (see more with '\''--help'\'')]' \
'-V[Print version]' \
'--version[Print version]' \
":: :_linear_commands" \
"*::: :->linear" \
&& ret=0
    case $state in
    (linear)
        words=($line[1] "${words[@]}")
        (( CURRENT += 1 ))
        curcontext="${curcontext%:*:*}:linear-command-$line[1]:"
        case $line[1] in
            (auth)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'-h[Print help]' \
'--help[Print help]' \
":: :_linear__subcmd__auth_commands" \
"*::: :->auth" \
&& ret=0

    case $state in
    (auth)
        words=($line[1] "${words[@]}")
        (( CURRENT += 1 ))
        curcontext="${curcontext%:*:*}:linear-auth-command-$line[1]:"
        case $line[1] in
            (login)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'-k+[API key (prompted for, or read from stdin when it is piped)]:key:_default' \
'--key=[API key (prompted for, or read from stdin when it is piped)]:key:_default' \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'--plaintext[Store API key in credentials file instead of system keyring]' \
'-h[Print help]' \
'--help[Print help]' \
&& ret=0
;;
(logout)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'-f[Skip confirmation prompt]' \
'--force[Skip confirmation prompt]' \
'-h[Print help]' \
'--help[Print help]' \
'::workspace_name:_default' \
&& ret=0
;;
(list)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'-h[Print help]' \
'--help[Print help]' \
&& ret=0
;;
(default)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'-h[Print help]' \
'--help[Print help]' \
'::workspace_name:_default' \
&& ret=0
;;
(token)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'-h[Print help]' \
'--help[Print help]' \
&& ret=0
;;
(whoami)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'-h[Print help]' \
'--help[Print help]' \
&& ret=0
;;
(migrate)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'-h[Print help]' \
'--help[Print help]' \
&& ret=0
;;
(help)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
":: :_linear__subcmd__auth__subcmd__help_commands" \
"*::: :->help" \
&& ret=0

    case $state in
    (help)
        words=($line[1] "${words[@]}")
        (( CURRENT += 1 ))
        curcontext="${curcontext%:*:*}:linear-auth-help-command-$line[1]:"
        case $line[1] in
            (login)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
&& ret=0
;;
(logout)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
&& ret=0
;;
(list)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
&& ret=0
;;
(default)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
&& ret=0
;;
(token)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
&& ret=0
;;
(whoami)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
&& ret=0
;;
(migrate)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
&& ret=0
;;
(help)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
&& ret=0
;;
        esac
    ;;
esac
;;
        esac
    ;;
esac
;;
(issue)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'-h[Print help]' \
'--help[Print help]' \
":: :_linear__subcmd__issue_commands" \
"*::: :->issue" \
&& ret=0

    case $state in
    (issue)
        words=($line[1] "${words[@]}")
        (( CURRENT += 1 ))
        curcontext="${curcontext%:*:*}:linear-issue-command-$line[1]:"
        case $line[1] in
            (id)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'-h[Print help]' \
'--help[Print help]' \
&& ret=0
;;
(mine)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'*-s+[Filter by workflow state type (triage, backlog, unstarted, started, completed, canceled), name, or ID (can be repeated for multiple states)]:state:_default' \
'*--state=[Filter by workflow state type (triage, backlog, unstarted, started, completed, canceled), name, or ID (can be repeated for multiple states)]:state:_default' \
'--sort=[Sort order (default\: priority, can also be set via LINEAR_ISSUE_SORT)]:sort:(manual priority)' \
'--team=[Team key, name, or ID to list issues for (if not your default team)]:team:_default' \
'--project=[Filter by project (UUID, slug ID, or name)]:project:_default' \
'(--project --milestone)--project-label=[Filter by project label name (shows issues from all projects with this label)]:projectLabel:_default' \
'--cycle=[Filter by cycle name, number, '\''active'\''/'\''now'\'', '\''next'\'', '\''previous'\'', or a relative offset like +1]:cycle:_default' \
'--milestone=[Filter by project milestone (UUID, or name when --project is set)]:milestone:_default' \
'*-l+[Filter by label name (can be repeated for multiple labels)]:label:_default' \
'*--label=[Filter by label name (can be repeated for multiple labels)]:label:_default' \
'--limit=[Maximum number of issues to fetch (default\: 50, use 0 for unlimited)]:limit:_default' \
'--created-after=[Filter issues created after this date (ISO 8601 or YYYY-MM-DD)]:date:_default' \
'--updated-after=[Filter issues updated after this date (ISO 8601 or YYYY-MM-DD)]:date:_default' \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'(-s --state)--all-states[Show issues from all states]' \
'-w[Open in web browser]' \
'--web[Open in web browser]' \
'-a[Open in Linear.app]' \
'--app[Open in Linear.app]' \
'--no-pager[Disable automatic paging for long output]' \
'-h[Print help]' \
'--help[Print help]' \
&& ret=0
;;
(list)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'*-s+[Filter by workflow state type (triage, backlog, unstarted, started, completed, canceled), name, or ID (can be repeated for multiple states)]:state:_default' \
'*--state=[Filter by workflow state type (triage, backlog, unstarted, started, completed, canceled), name, or ID (can be repeated for multiple states)]:state:_default' \
'--sort=[Sort order (default\: priority, can also be set via LINEAR_ISSUE_SORT)]:sort:(manual priority)' \
'--team=[Team key, name, or ID to list issues for (if not your default team)]:team:_default' \
'--project=[Filter by project (UUID, slug ID, or name)]:project:_default' \
'(--project --milestone)--project-label=[Filter by project label name (shows issues from all projects with this label)]:projectLabel:_default' \
'--cycle=[Filter by cycle name, number, '\''active'\''/'\''now'\'', '\''next'\'', '\''previous'\'', or a relative offset like +1]:cycle:_default' \
'--milestone=[Filter by project milestone (UUID, or name when --project is set)]:milestone:_default' \
'*-l+[Filter by label name (can be repeated for multiple labels)]:label:_default' \
'*--label=[Filter by label name (can be repeated for multiple labels)]:label:_default' \
'--limit=[Maximum number of issues to fetch (default\: 50, use 0 for unlimited)]:limit:_default' \
'--created-after=[Filter issues created after this date (ISO 8601 or YYYY-MM-DD)]:date:_default' \
'--updated-after=[Filter issues updated after this date (ISO 8601 or YYYY-MM-DD)]:date:_default' \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'(-s --state)--all-states[Show issues from all states]' \
'-w[Open in web browser]' \
'--web[Open in web browser]' \
'-a[Open in Linear.app]' \
'--app[Open in Linear.app]' \
'--no-pager[Disable automatic paging for long output]' \
'-h[Print help]' \
'--help[Print help]' \
&& ret=0
;;
(l)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'*-s+[Filter by workflow state type (triage, backlog, unstarted, started, completed, canceled), name, or ID (can be repeated for multiple states)]:state:_default' \
'*--state=[Filter by workflow state type (triage, backlog, unstarted, started, completed, canceled), name, or ID (can be repeated for multiple states)]:state:_default' \
'--sort=[Sort order (default\: priority, can also be set via LINEAR_ISSUE_SORT)]:sort:(manual priority)' \
'--team=[Team key, name, or ID to list issues for (if not your default team)]:team:_default' \
'--project=[Filter by project (UUID, slug ID, or name)]:project:_default' \
'(--project --milestone)--project-label=[Filter by project label name (shows issues from all projects with this label)]:projectLabel:_default' \
'--cycle=[Filter by cycle name, number, '\''active'\''/'\''now'\'', '\''next'\'', '\''previous'\'', or a relative offset like +1]:cycle:_default' \
'--milestone=[Filter by project milestone (UUID, or name when --project is set)]:milestone:_default' \
'*-l+[Filter by label name (can be repeated for multiple labels)]:label:_default' \
'*--label=[Filter by label name (can be repeated for multiple labels)]:label:_default' \
'--limit=[Maximum number of issues to fetch (default\: 50, use 0 for unlimited)]:limit:_default' \
'--created-after=[Filter issues created after this date (ISO 8601 or YYYY-MM-DD)]:date:_default' \
'--updated-after=[Filter issues updated after this date (ISO 8601 or YYYY-MM-DD)]:date:_default' \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'(-s --state)--all-states[Show issues from all states]' \
'-w[Open in web browser]' \
'--web[Open in web browser]' \
'-a[Open in Linear.app]' \
'--app[Open in Linear.app]' \
'--no-pager[Disable automatic paging for long output]' \
'-h[Print help]' \
'--help[Print help]' \
&& ret=0
;;
(query)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'--search=[Full-text search term]:term:_default' \
'*--team=[Filter by team key, name, or ID (can be repeated for multiple teams)]:team:_default' \
'*-s+[Filter by workflow state type (triage, backlog, unstarted, started, completed, canceled), name, or ID (can be repeated for multiple states)]:state:_default' \
'*--state=[Filter by workflow state type (triage, backlog, unstarted, started, completed, canceled), name, or ID (can be repeated for multiple states)]:state:_default' \
'--assignee=[Filter by assignee (username)]:assignee:_default' \
'(--search)--sort=[Sort order\: manual or priority (default\: priority, not available with --search)]:sort:(manual priority)' \
'--project=[Filter by project (UUID, slug ID, or name)]:project:_default' \
'(--project --milestone)--project-label=[Filter by project label name (shows issues from all projects with this label)]:projectLabel:_default' \
'--cycle=[Filter by cycle name, number, '\''active'\''/'\''now'\'', '\''next'\'', '\''previous'\'', or a relative offset like +1]:cycle:_default' \
'--milestone=[Filter by project milestone (UUID, or name when --project is set)]:milestone:_default' \
'*-l+[Filter by label name (can be repeated for multiple labels)]:label:_default' \
'*--label=[Filter by label name (can be repeated for multiple labels)]:label:_default' \
'--limit=[Maximum number of issues to fetch (default\: 50, use 0 for unlimited)]:limit:_default' \
'--created-after=[Filter issues created after this date (ISO 8601 or YYYY-MM-DD)]:date:_default' \
'--updated-after=[Filter issues updated after this date (ISO 8601 or YYYY-MM-DD)]:date:_default' \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'--search-comments[Also search inside issue comments (requires --search)]' \
'(--team)--all-teams[Query across all teams]' \
'(-s --state)--all-states[Show issues from all states (this is the default)]' \
'(--assignee -U --unassigned)-A[Show issues for all assignees (this is the default)]' \
'(--assignee -U --unassigned)--all-assignees[Show issues for all assignees (this is the default)]' \
'(--assignee)-U[Show only unassigned issues]' \
'(--assignee)--unassigned[Show only unassigned issues]' \
'--include-archived[Include archived issues]' \
'-j[Output results as JSON]' \
'--json[Output results as JSON]' \
'--no-pager[Disable automatic paging for long output]' \
'-h[Print help]' \
'--help[Print help]' \
&& ret=0
;;
(q)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'--search=[Full-text search term]:term:_default' \
'*--team=[Filter by team key, name, or ID (can be repeated for multiple teams)]:team:_default' \
'*-s+[Filter by workflow state type (triage, backlog, unstarted, started, completed, canceled), name, or ID (can be repeated for multiple states)]:state:_default' \

(create)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'-a+[Assign the issue to '\''self'\'' or someone (by username or name)]:assignee:_default' \
'--assignee=[Assign the issue to '\''self'\'' or someone (by username or name)]:assignee:_default' \
'--due-date=[Due date of the issue]:dueDate:_default' \
'--parent=[Parent issue (if any) as a team_number code]:parent:_default' \
'-p+[Priority of the issue (1-4, descending priority)]:priority:_default' \
'--priority=[Priority of the issue (1-4, descending priority)]:priority:_default' \
'--estimate=[Points estimate of the issue]:estimate:_default' \
'-d+[Description of the issue]:description:_default' \
'--description=[Description of the issue]:description:_default' \
'--description-file=[Read description from a file (preferred for markdown content)]:path:_default' \
'*-l+[Issue label associated with the issue. May be repeated.]:label:_default' \
'*--label=[Issue label associated with the issue. May be repeated.]:label:_default' \
'--team=[Team (key, name, or ID) for the issue, if not your default team]:team:_default' \
'--project=[Project for the issue (UUID, slug ID, or name)]:project:_default' \
'-s+[Workflow state for the issue (by name or type)]:state:_default' \
'--state=[Workflow state for the issue (by name or type)]:state:_default' \
'--milestone=[Project milestone (UUID, or name when --project is set)]:milestone:_default' \
'--cycle=[Cycle name, number, '\''active'\''/'\''now'\'', '\''next'\'', '\''previous'\'', or a relative offset like +1 (use --cycle=-1 for negatives)]:cycle:_default' \
'--template=[Issue template to apply, by name or ID (the team'\''s templates plus workspace ones). Takes the place of the team'\''s default template. The template fills in anything you do not pass\: explicit flags override it, --label merges with the template'\''s labels, and --description replaces the template body (omit it to keep the body). Makes --title optional.]:template:_default' \
'-t+[Title of the issue]:title:_default' \
'--title=[Title of the issue]:title:_default' \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'--start[Start the issue after creation]' \
'--no-use-default-template[Do not use default template for the issue]' \
'--no-interactive[Disable interactive prompts]' \
'-h[Print help (see more with '\''--help'\'')]' \
'--help[Print help (see more with '\''--help'\'')]' \
&& ret=0
;;
(update)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'-a+[Assign the issue to '\''self'\'' or someone (by username or name)]:assignee:_default' \
'--assignee=[Assign the issue to '\''self'\'' or someone (by username or name)]:assignee:_default' \
'--due-date=[Due date of the issue. Use --clear-due-date to remove it]:dueDate:_default' \
'--parent=[Parent issue (if any) as a team_number code. Use --clear-parent to remove it]:parent:_default' \
'-p+[Priority of the issue (1-4, descending priority)]:priority:_default' \
'--priority=[Priority of the issue (1-4, descending priority)]:priority:_default' \
'--estimate=[Points estimate of the issue. Use --clear-estimate to remove it]:estimate:_default' \
'-d+[Description of the issue]:description:_default' \
'--description=[Description of the issue]:description:_default' \
'--description-file=[Read description from a file (preferred for markdown content)]:path:_default' \
'*-l+[Issue label associated with the issue; replaces the issue'\''s entire label set. May be repeated. Use --add-label/--remove-label to change labels incrementally.]:label:_default' \
'*--label=[Issue label associated with the issue; replaces the issue'\''s entire label set. May be repeated. Use --add-label/--remove-label to change labels incrementally.]:label:_default' \
'*--add-label=[Add a label to the issue, keeping its existing labels. May be repeated.]:label:_default' \
'*--remove-label=[Remove a label from the issue, keeping its other labels (does not delete the label from the team). May be repeated.]:label:_default' \
'--team=[Team (key, name, or ID) to move the issue to]:team:_default' \
'--project=[Project to assign the issue to (UUID, slug ID, or name). Use --clear-project to remove it]:project:_default' \
'-s+[Workflow state for the issue (by name or type)]:state:_default' \
'--state=[Workflow state for the issue (by name or type)]:state:_default' \
'--milestone=[Project milestone (UUID, or name when --project is set or the issue already has a project). Use --clear-milestone to remove it]:milestone:_default' \
'--cycle=[Cycle name, number, '\''active'\''/'\''now'\'', '\''next'\'', '\''previous'\'', or a relative offset like +1 (use --cycle=-1 for negatives). Use --clear-cycle to remove the issue from its cycle]:cycle:_default' \
'-t+[Title of the issue]:title:_default' \
'--title=[Title of the issue]:title:_default' \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'--unassign[Clear the issue'\''s assignee (cannot be combined with --assignee)]' \
'--clear-due-date[Remove the issue'\''s due date (cannot be combined with --due-date)]' \
'--clear-parent[Remove the issue'\''s parent (cannot be combined with --parent)]' \
'--clear-estimate[Remove the issue'\''s estimate (cannot be combined with --estimate)]' \
'--clear-project[Remove the issue from its project (cannot be combined with --project or --milestone)]' \
'--clear-milestone[Remove the issue from its project milestone (cannot be combined with --milestone)]' \
'--clear-cycle[Remove the issue from its cycle]' \
'-h[Print help (see more with '\''--help'\'')]' \
'--help[Print help (see more with '\''--help'\'')]' \
'::issue_id:_default' \
&& ret=0

    (comment)
        words=($line[1] "${words[@]}")
        (( CURRENT += 1 ))
        curcontext="${curcontext%:*:*}:linear-issue-comment-command-$line[1]:"
        case $line[1] in
            (add)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \

'*--attach=[Upload a file and add its Markdown link to the comment (images render inline; repeatable)]:filepath:_default' \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'--public[Upload attached images to a public, unauthenticated URL (default\: private, workspace-members only)]' \

'--icon=[Project icon]:icon:_default' \
'--color=[Project color as a HEX string]:color:_default' \
'--initiative=[Add to initiative immediately (ID, slug, or name)]:initiative:_default' \
'--template=[Project template to apply, by name or ID (workspace templates plus those of the project'\''s teams). The template fills in anything you do not pass; explicit flags override it. Applied on create only.]:template:_default' \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'-i[Interactive mode (default if no flags provided)]' \
'--interactive[Interactive mode (default if no flags provided)]' \
'-j[Output created project as JSON]' \
'--json[Output created project as JSON]' \

&& ret=0

'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
&& ret=0
;;
        esac
    ;;
esac
;;
(help)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
&& ret=0
;;
        esac
    ;;
esac
;;
        esac
    ;;
esac
;;
(docs)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'-h[Print help]' \
'--help[Print help]' \
":: :_linear__subcmd__document_commands" \
"*::: :->document" \
&& ret=0

    case $state in
    (document)
        words=($line[1] "${words[@]}")
        (( CURRENT += 1 ))
        curcontext="${curcontext%:*:*}:linear-document-command-$line[1]:"
        case $line[1] in
            (list)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'(--issue --initiative --team --cycle --release)--project=[Filter by project (UUID, slug ID, or name)]:project:_default' \
'(--initiative --team --cycle --release)--issue=[Filter by issue (identifier like TC-123)]:issue:_default' \
'(--team --cycle --release)--initiative=[Filter by initiative (UUID, slug ID, or name)]:initiative:_default' \
'(--release)--team=[Filter by team (key, name, or ID); with --cycle, scopes the cycle lookup instead]:team:_default' \
'(--release)--cycle=[Filter by cycle\: name, number, '\''active'\''/'\''now'\'', '\''next'\'', '\''previous'\'', or a relative offset like +1 (team from --team or config)]:cycle:_default' \
'--release=[Filter by release (UUID, name, or version)]:release:_default' \
'--limit=[Limit results]:limit:_default' \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'--json[Output as JSON]' \
'-h[Print help]' \
'--help[Print help]' \
&& ret=0
;;
(l)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'(--issue --initiative --team --cycle --release)--project=[Filter by project (UUID, slug ID, or name)]:project:_default' \
'(--initiative --team --cycle --release)--issue=[Filter by issue (identifier like TC-123)]:issue:_default' \
'(--team --cycle --release)--initiative=[Filter by initiative (UUID, slug ID, or name)]:initiative:_default' \

'(--release)--cycle=[Filter by cycle\: name, number, '\''active'\''/'\''now'\'', '\''next'\'', '\''previous'\'', or a relative offset like +1 (team from --team or config)]:cycle:_default' \
'--release=[Filter by release (UUID, name, or version)]:release:_default' \
'--limit=[Limit results]:limit:_default' \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'--json[Output as JSON]' \
'-h[Print help]' \
'--help[Print help]' \
&& ret=0
;;
(view)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \

'(--team --cycle --release)--initiative=[Attach to initiative (UUID, slug ID, or name)]:initiative:_default' \

'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'(--project --issue --initiative --team --cycle --release)-i[Interactive mode with prompts]' \
'(--project --issue --initiative --team --cycle --release)--interactive[Interactive mode with prompts]' \
'-h[Print help (see more with '\''--help'\'')]' \
'--help[Print help (see more with '\''--help'\'')]' \

&& ret=0

    case $state in
    (comment)
        words=($line[1] "${words[@]}")
        (( CURRENT += 1 ))
        curcontext="${curcontext%:*:*}:linear-document-comment-command-$line[1]:"
        case $line[1] in
            (add)
_arguments "${_arguments_options[@]}" : \
'-b+[Comment body text]:text:_default' \
'--body=[Comment body text]:text:_default' \
'--body-file=[Read comment body from a file (preferred for markdown content)]:path:_default' \
'-p+[Reply to a top-level comment by ID (the reply joins that thread)]:commentId:_default' \
'--parent=[Reply to a top-level comment by ID (the reply joins that thread)]:commentId:_default' \
'--reply-to=[Reply to a top-level comment by ID (the reply joins that thread)]:commentId:_default' \
'--workspace=[Target workspace (uses credentials)]:slug:_default' \
'-h[Print help (see more with '\''--help'\'')]' \
'--help[Print help (see more with '\''--help'\'')]' \
':document:_default' \
&& ret=0

(( $+functions[_linear_commands] )) ||
_linear_commands() {
    local commands; commands=(
'auth:Manage Linear authentication' \
'issue:Manage Linear issues' \
'i:Manage Linear issues' \
'team:Manage Linear teams' \
't:Manage Linear teams' \
'user:Manage Linear users' \
'u:Manage Linear users' \
'project:Manage Linear projects' \
'p:Manage Linear projects' \
'project-update:Manage project status updates' \
'pu:Manage project status updates' \
'cycle:Manage Linear team cycles' \
'cy:Manage Linear team cycles' \
'milestone:Manage Linear project milestones' \
'm:Manage Linear project milestones' \
'initiative:Manage Linear initiatives' \
'init:Manage Linear initiatives' \
'initiative-update:Manage initiative status updates (timeline posts)' \
'iu:Manage initiative status updates (timeline posts)' \
'label:Manage Linear issue labels' \
'l:Manage Linear issue labels' \
'template:Browse Linear issue, project, and document templates. Apply one with \`issue create --template\` or \`project create --template\`.' \
'document:Manage Linear documents' \
'docs:Manage Linear documents' \
'doc:Manage Linear documents' \
'completions:Generate shell completions.' \
'config:Interactively generate .linear.toml configuration' \
'configure:Interactively generate .linear.toml configuration' \
'schema:Print the GraphQL schema to stdout' \
'api:Make a raw GraphQL API request' \
'markdown:Linear-flavored Markdown\: mentions and collapsible sections' \
'help:Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)' \
    )
    _describe -t commands 'linear commands' commands "$@"
}

_linear__subcmd__issue_commands() {
    local commands; commands=(
'id:Print the issue based on the current git branch' \
'mine:List your issues' \
'list:List your issues' \
'l:List your issues' \
'query:Query issues with structured filters' \
'q:Query issues with structured filters' \
'title:Print the issue title' \
'start:Start working on an issue' \
'view:View issue details (default) or open in browser/app' \
'v:View issue details (default) or open in browser/app' \
'url:Print the issue URL' \
'describe:Print the issue title and Linear-issue trailer' \
'commits:Show all commits for a Linear issue (jj only)' \
'pull-request:Create a GitHub pull request with issue details' \
'pr:Create a GitHub pull request with issue details' \
'archive:Archive an issue' \
'delete:Delete an issue' \
'd:Delete an issue' \
'create:Create a linear issue' \
'update:Update a linear issue' \
'comment:Manage issue comments' \
'attach:Create a sidebar link attachment on an issue (images do not render inline)' \
'link:Link a URL to an issue' \
'relation:Manage issue relations (dependencies)' \
'agent-session:Manage agent sessions for an issue' \
'help:Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)' \
    )
    _describe -t commands 'linear issue commands' commands "$@"
}

_linear__subcmd__issue__subcmd__help_commands() {
    local commands; commands=(
'id:Print the issue based on the current git branch' \
'mine:List your issues' \
'query:Query issues with structured filters' \
'title:Print the issue title' \
'start:Start working on an issue' \
'view:View issue details (default) or open in browser/app' \
'url:Print the issue URL' \
'describe:Print the issue title and Linear-issue trailer' \
'commits:Show all commits for a Linear issue (jj only)' \
'pull-request:Create a GitHub pull request with issue details' \
'archive:Archive an issue' \
'delete:Delete an issue' \
'create:Create a linear issue' \
'update:Update a linear issue' \
'comment:Manage issue comments' \
'attach:Create a sidebar link attachment on an issue (images do not render inline)' \
'link:Link a URL to an issue' \
'relation:Manage issue relations (dependencies)' \
'agent-session:Manage agent sessions for an issue' \
'help:Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)' \
    )
    _describe -t commands 'linear issue help commands' commands "$@"
}

    local commands; commands=(
'list:List projects' \
'view:View project details' \
'v:View project details' \
'create:Create a new Linear project' \
'update:Update a Linear project' \
'delete:Delete (trash) a Linear project' \
'comment:Manage project comments' \
'help:Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)' \
    )
    _describe -t commands 'linear project commands' commands "$@"
}
